}\n248\t        }\n249\t\n250\t        if (!canSplit) {\n251\t            triangleIndices = indices;\n252\t            return;\n253\t        }\n254\t\n255\t        // Build children\n256\t        for false;\n284\t\n285\t        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;\n286\t        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;\n287\t        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n288\t\n289\t        return true;\n290\t    }\n291\t\n292\t    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n293\t    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n294\t    template<typename Func>\n295\t    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n296\t        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n297\t        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {\n298\t            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\n299\t                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\n300\t            }\n301\t            return false;\n302\t        }\n303\t\n304\t        // Otherwise, descend to children\n305\t        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n306\t            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\n307\t                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;\n308\t            }\n309\t        }\n310\t        return false;\n311\t    }\n312\t};\n313\t\n314\t// ============================================================================\n315\t// Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n316\t// ============================================================================\n317\t\n318\t// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\n319\tclass IcosphereMesh {\n320\tpublic:\n321\t    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n322\t\n323\t    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n324\t        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n325\t        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n326\t\n327\t        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\n328\t            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n329\t            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n330\t            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n331\t            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n332\t            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n333\t            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n334\t            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n335\t            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n336\t            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n337\t            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,\n338\t            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n339\t            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\n340\t        };\n341\t\n342\t        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\n343\t        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\n344\t            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n345\t            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n346\t            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},\n347\t            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}\n348\t        };\n349\t\n350\t        // Subdivide\n351\t        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\n352\t            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n353\t            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n354\t\n355\t            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n356\t                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n357\t                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n358\t                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n359\t\n360\t                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\n361\t                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\n362\t                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\n363\t                vertices.push_back(mid);\n364\t                midpointCache[key] = idx;\n365\t                return idx;\n366\t            };\n367\t\n368\t            for (const auto& face : faces) {\n369\t                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);\n370\t                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);\n371\t                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);\n372\t\n373\t                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\n374\t                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\n375\t                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\n376\t                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});\n377\t            }\n378\t            faces = std::move(newFaces);\n379\t        }\n380\t\n381\t        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \"room\")\n382\t        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\n383\t        for (const auto& face : faces) {\n384\t            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n385\t            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n386\t        }\n387\t    }\n388\t};\n389\t\n390\t// ============================================================================\n391\t// Random Number Generation\n392\t// ============================================================================\n393\t\n394\tclass RandomGenerator {\n395\t    std::mt19937 rng;\n396\t    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\n397\tpublic:\n398\t    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n399\t    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\n400\t};\n401\t\n402\t// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\n403\tVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n404\t    val_t u = rng.rand();\n405\t    val_t v = rng.rand();\n406\t    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n407\t        u = 1.0f - u;\n408\t        v = 1.0f - v;\n409\t    }\n410\t    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n411\t    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n412\t    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n413\t}\n414\t\n415\t// ============================================================================\n416\t// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n417\t// ============================================================================\n418\t\n419\tval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n420\t                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n421\t    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n422\t    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n423\t    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n424\t    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n425\t\n426\t    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n427\t\n428\t    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n429\t    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n430\t    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n431\t    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n432\t\n433\t    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n434\t    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n435\t    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n436\t\n437\t    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n438\t}\n439\t\n440\t// ============================================================================\n441\t// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n442\t// ============================================================================\n443\t\n444\t// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n445\t// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\n446\tbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n447\t                  const Octree& octree,\n448\t                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n449\t    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n450\t    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n451\t    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n452\t    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n453\t\n454\t    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n455\t        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n456\t\n457\t        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n458\t        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n459\t            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n460\t        }\n461\t        return false;\n462\t    });\n463\t}\n464\t\n465\t// ============================================================================\n466\t// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n467\t// ============================================================================\n468\t\n469\t// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\n470\tval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n471\t    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n472\t    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n473\t    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n474\t}\n475\t\n476\t// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\n477\tval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n478\t                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n479\t                  const Octree& octree,\n480\t                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n481\t    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n482\t    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n483\t\n484\t    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\n485\t    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\n486\t\n487\t    val_t kij = ZERO;\n488\t\n489\t    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n490\t        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n491\t        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n492\t\n493\t        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n494\t\n495\t        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n496\t        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n497\t        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n498\t\n499\t        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n500\t        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n501\t\n502\t        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n503\t\n504\t        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n505\t    }\n506\t\n507\t    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n508\t}\n509\t\n510\t// ============================================================================\n511\t// Tau (time delay) Computation\n512\t// ============================================================================\n513\t\n514\tint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n515\t    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n516\t    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n517\t}\n518\t\n519\t// ============================================================================\n520\t// Room Response Simulation State\n521\t// ============================================================================\n522\t\n523\tstruct SimulationState {\n524\t    size_t numTriangles;\n525\t    size_t numTimesteps;\n526\t\n527\t    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n528\t    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\n529\t    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n530\t    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)\n531\t    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)\n532\t    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n533\t    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n534\t    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n535\t\n536\t    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n537\t\n538\t    size_t sourceIndex;\n539\t\n540\t    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\n541\t    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n542\t};\n543\t\n544\t// ============================================================================\n545\t// Initialization\n546\t// ============================================================================\n547\t\n548\tvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n549\t                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n550\t    // Generate mesh\n551\t    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n552\t    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n553\t    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n554\t    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n555\t    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n556\t\n557\t    printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n558\t\n559\t    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n560\t    printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n561\t    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n562\t\n563\t    // Initialize areas\n564\t    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n565\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n566\t        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n567\t    }\n568\t\n569\t    // Initialize reflectivity\n570\t    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n571\t\n572\t    // Initialize matrices\n573\t    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n574\t    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);\n575\t    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n576\t    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n577\t    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n578\t\n579\t    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n580\t    size_t timeOn = 0;\n581\t    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n582\t    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n583\t        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n584\t    }\n585\t}\n586\t\n587\t// ============================================================================\n588\t// Precomputation Phase\n589\t// ============================================================================\n590\t\n591\tvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n592\t    printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n593\t    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n594\t\n595\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n596\t        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n597\t            if (i == j) continue;\n598\t            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\n599\t                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n600\t        }\n601\t        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\n602\t            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\n603\t        }\n604\t    }\n605\t}\n606\t\n607\tvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n608\t    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n609\t\n610\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n611\t        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n612\t            if (i == j) continue;\n613\t            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(\n614\t                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\n615\t        }\n616\t    }\n617\t}\n618\t\n619\t// ============================================================================\n620\t// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n621\t// ============================================================================\n622\t\n623\tvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n624\t    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n625\t\n626\t    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n627\t        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n628\t            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n629\t\n630\t            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n631\t                if (i == j) continue;\n632\t\n633\t                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n634\t\n635\t                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i\n636\t                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\n637\t\n638\t                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n639\t                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n640\t\n641\t                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred\n642\t                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);\n643\t                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];\n644\t                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;\n645\t\n646\t                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n647\t                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n648\t            }\n649\t\n650\t            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission\n651\t            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];\n652\t        }\n653\t\n654\t        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {\n655\t            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n656\t        }\n657\t    }\n658\t}\n659\t\n660\t// ============================================================================\n661\t// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)\n662\t// ============================================================================\n663\t\n664\tvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n665\t    printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n666\t\n667\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n668\t        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n669\t        int bestT = 0;\n670\t\n671\t        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n672\t        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n673\t            val_t sum = ZERO;\n674\t\n675\t            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\n676\t                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n677\t                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];\n678\t                sum += pS * pB;\n679\t            }\n680\t\n681\t            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n682\t                maxCorr = sum;\n683\t                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n684\t            }\n685\t        }\n686\t\n687\t        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n688\t    }\n689\t}\n690\t\n691\t// ============================================================================\n692\t// Validation\n693\t// ============================================================================\n694\t\n695\tbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n696\t    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n697\t\n698\t    // Check that distances are non-negative\n699\t    bool allNonNegative = true;\n700\t    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n701\t    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n702\t    val_t sumDist = ZERO;\n703\t    int nonZeroCount = 0;\n704\t\n705\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n706\t        val_t d = state.distances[i];\n707\t        if (d < 0) {\n708\t            allNonNegative = false;\n709\t            printf(\"  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n710\t        }\n711\t        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {\n712\t            printf(\"  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\\n\", i, d);\n713\t            return false;\n714\t        }\n715\t        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);\n716\t        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);\n717\t        sumDist += d;\n718\t        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;\n719\t    }\n720\t\n721\t    printf(\"  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\\n\", minDist, maxDist);\n722\t    printf(\"  Average distance: %.4f\\n\", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));\n723\t    printf(\"  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\\n\", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);\n724\t\n725\t    // Check source distance is zero or very small\n726\t    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];\n727\t    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {\n728\t        printf(\"  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\\n\", srcDist);\n729\t    }\n730\t\n731\t    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)\n732\t    int receivedEnergy = 0;\n733\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n734\t        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n735\t            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {\n736\t                receivedEnergy++;\n737\t                break;\n738\t            }\n739\t        }\n740\t    }\n741\t\n742\t    printf(\"  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\\n\", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);\n743\t\n744\t    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {\n745\t        printf(\"  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\\n\");\n746\t        return false;\n747\t    }\n748\t\n749\t    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)\n750\t    int nonZeroKij = 0;\n751\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n752\t        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) nonZeroKij++;\n753\t    }\n754\t    printf(\"  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\\n\",\n755\t           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,\n756\t           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));\n757\t\n758\t    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {\n759\t        printf(\"  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\\n\");\n760\t        return false;\n761\t    }\n762\t\n763\t    if (!allNonNegative) {\n764\t        return false;\n765\t    }\n766\t\n767\t    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n768\t    return true;\n769\t}\n770\t\n771\t// ============================================================================\n772\t// Hash for Verification\n773\t// ============================================================================\n774\t\n775\tuint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {\n776\t    uint64_t hash = 0;\n777\t    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n778\t        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);\n779\t        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n780\t    }\n781\t    return hash;\n782\t}\n783\t\n784\t// ============================================================================\n785\t// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count\n786\t// ============================================================================\n787\t\n788\tint getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {\n789\t    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision\n790\t    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480\n791\t    int subdivisions = 0;\n792\t    int triangles = 20;\n793\t    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {\n794\t        subdivisions++;\n795\t        triangles *= 4;\n796\t    }\n797\t    return subdivisions;\n798\t}\n799\t\n800\t// ============================================================================\n801\t// Main\n802\t// ============================================================================\n803\t\n804\tvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n805\t    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n806\t    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n807\t    printf(\"  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\\n\");\n808\t    printf(\"               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\\n\");\n809\t    printf(\"               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\\n\");\n810\t    printf(\"  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\\n\");\n811\t    printf(\"  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\\n\");\n812\t    printf(\"  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 * Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n * \n * This is a simplified, purely sequential implementation of room impulse response\n * simulation using radiosity-based wave propagation. It models how sound/light\n * waves propagate between surfaces in a room by computing:\n * \n * 1. Form factors (Kij) between all pairs of triangles based on visibility and geometry\n * 2. Wave propagation using radiosity equations with time delays (Tau)\n * 3. Distance estimation via cross-correlation of radiosity values\n * \n * The implementation generates a procedural icosphere mesh to simulate a room.\n */\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include 0.0f;\nconstexpr val_t ONE = 1.0f;\nconstexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)\nconstexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;\nconstexpr int ============================================================================\n// Vector and Triangle Types\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    val_t x, y, z;\n\n    constexpr Vec3() : x(0), y(0), z(0) {}\n    constexpr Vec3(val_t x, val_t y, val_t z) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n    explicit constexpr Vec3(val_t v) : x(v), y(v), z(v) {}\n\n    Vec3 operator+(const Vec3& o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }\n    Vec3 operator-(const Vec3& o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }\n    Vec3 operator*(val_t s) const { return {x * s, y * s, z * s}; }\n    Vec3 operator/(val_t s) const { return {x / s, y / s, z / s}; }\n    Vec3 operator-() const { return {-x, -y, -z}; }\n\n    val_t dot(const Vec3& o) const { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }\n    Vec3 cross(const Vec3& o) const {\n        return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x};\n    }\n\n    val_t squaredNorm() const { return x * x + y * y + z * z; }\n    val_t norm() const { return std::sqrt(squaredNorm()); }\n    Vec3 normalized() const {\n        val_t n = norm();\n        return n > EPSILON ? *this / n : Vec3();\n    }\n\n    bool operator==(const Vec3& o) const {\n        return std::abs(x - o.x) < EPSILON && std::abs(y - o.y) < EPSILON && std::abs(z - o.z) < EPSILON;\n    }\n};\n\nstruct Triangle {\n    Vec3 a, b, c;\n    Vec3 _normal;\n\n    Triangle() = default;\n    Triangle(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, const Vec3& c)\n        : a(a), b(b), c(c), _normal((b - a).cross(c - a).normalized()) {}\n\n    Vec3 center() const { return (a + b + c) / 3.0f; }\n    Vec3 normal() const { return _normal; }\n\n    val_t area() const {\n        Vec3 ab = b - a;\n        Vec3 ac = c - a;\n        return 0.5f * ab.cross(ac).norm();\n    }\n\n    bool operator==(const Triangle& o) const { return a == o.a && b == o.b && c == o.c; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap\nbool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {\n    // Translate triangle to box center\n    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;\n    Vec3 v1 = tri.b - boxCenter;\n    Vec3 v2 = tri.c - boxCenter;\n\n    // Triangle edges\n    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;\n    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;\n\n    // Test box normals (AABB axes)\n    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {\n        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));\n    };\n\n    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);\n    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return false;\n\n    auto [minY, maxY] = minMax3(v0.y, v1.y, v2.y);\n    if (minY > boxHalfSize.y || maxY < -boxHalfSize.y) return false;\n\n    auto [minZ, maxZ] = minMax3(v0.z, v1.z, v2.z);\n    if (minZ > boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;\n\n    // Test triangle normal\n    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);\n    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);\n    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +\n              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +\n              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);\n    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;\n\n    // Test 9 edge cross products\n    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {\n        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);\n        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);\n        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);\n        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +\n                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +\n                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);\n        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);\n        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);\n    };\n\n    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};\n    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};\n    for (const auto& axis : axes) {\n        for (const auto& edge : edges) {\n            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);\n            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {\n                if (!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Octree for Spatial Acceleration\n// ============================================================================\n\nconstexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;\nconstexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;\n\nclass Octree {\npublic:\n    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;\n    Vec3 halfExtent, center;\n    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];\n    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list\n    const std::vector<Triangle>* allTriangles;  // Pointer to all triangles\n\n    Octree() : allTriangles(nullptr) {}\n\n    void build(const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles) {\n        allTriangles = &triangles;\n        if (triangles.empty()) return;\n\n        // Compute bounding box\n        minBound = triangles[0].a;\n        maxBound = triangles[0].a;\n        for (const auto& tri : triangles) {\n            for (const auto* v : {&tri.a, &tri.b, &tri.c}) {\n                minBound.x = std::min(minBound.x, v->x);\n                minBound.y = std::min(minBound.y, v->y);\n                minBound.z = std::min(minBound.z, v->z);\n                maxBound.x = std::max(maxBound.x, v->x);\n                maxBound.y = std::max(maxBound.y, v->y);\n                maxBound.z = std::max(maxBound.z, v->z);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Collect all indices\n        std::vector<size_t> allIndices(triangles.size());\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < triangles.size(); ++i) allIndices[i] = i;\n\n        buildNode(allIndices, minBound, maxBound);\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    void buildNode(const std::vector<size_t>& indices, const Vec3& nodeMin, const Vec3& nodeMax) {\n        minBound = nodeMin;\n        maxBound = nodeMax;\n        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;\n        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;\n\n        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf\n        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\n            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Subdivide into 8 children\n        Vec3 childHalfSize = halfExtent * 0.5f;\n        std::vector<size_t> childIndices[8];\n\n        for (size_t idx : indices) {\n            const Triangle& tri = (*allTriangles)[idx];\n\n            // Check which children this triangle overlaps\n            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n                Vec3 childCenter = center;\n                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;\n                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;\n                childCenter.z += (i & 4) ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;\n\n                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {\n                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)\n        bool canSplit = false;\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {\n                canSplit = true;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (!canSplit) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Build children\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (!childIndices[i].empty()) {\n                Vec3 childMin = center;\n                Vec3 childMax = center;\n                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;\n                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;\n                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;\n                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;\n                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;\n                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : center.z;\n\n                children[i] = std::make_unique<Octree>();\n                children[i]->allTriangles = allTriangles;\n                children[i]->buildNode(childIndices[i], childMin, childMax);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\npublic:\n    // Check if a ray intersects this node's bounding box\n    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {\n        Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;\n        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;\n        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};\n\n        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\n\n        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n    template<typename Func>\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {\n            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\n                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\n            }\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Otherwise, descend to children\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\n                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\nclass IcosphereMesh {\npublic:\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\n            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\n        };\n\n        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\n        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},\n            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}\n        };\n\n        // Subdivide\n        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\n            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\n                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\n                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\n                vertices.push_back(mid);\n                midpointCache[key] = idx;\n                return idx;\n            };\n\n            for (const auto& face : faces) {\n                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);\n                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);\n                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);\n\n                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\n                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});\n            }\n            faces = std::move(newFaces);\n        }\n\n        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \"room\")\n        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\n        for (const auto& face : faces) {\n            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Random Number Generation\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass RandomGenerator {\n    std::mt19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\npublic:\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\n};\n\n// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n        u = 1.0f - u;\n        v = 1.0f - v;\n    }\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n// ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n row-major)\n    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\n                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n        }\n        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(\n                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\n                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n ============================================================================\n// Validation\n// ============================================================================\n\nbool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist = { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }\n    Vec3 cross(const Vec3& o) const {\n        return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x};\n    }\n\n    val_t squaredNorm() const { {\n    Vec3 a, b, c;\n    Vec3 _normal;\n\n    Triangle() = default;\n    Triangle(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, const Vec3& c)\n        : a(a), b(b), c(c), _normal((b - a).cross(c - a).normalized()) {}\n\n    Vec3 center() const { return (a + b + c) / 3.0f; }\n    Vec3 normal() const { return _normal; }\n\n    val_t area() const {\n        Vec3 ab = b - a;\n        Vec3 ac = c - a;\n        return 0.5f * ab.cross(ac).norm();\n    }\n\n    bool operator==(const Triangle& o) const { return a == o.a && b == o.b && c == o.c; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap\nbool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {\n    // Translate triangle to box center\n    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;\n    Vec3 v1 = tri.b - boxCenter;\n    Vec3 v2 = tri.c - boxCenter;\n\n    // Triangle edges\n    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;\n    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;\n\n    // Test box normals (AABB axes)\n    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {\n        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));\n    };\n\n    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);\n    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return false;\n\n    auto [minY, maxY] = minMax3(v0.y, v1.y, v2.y);\n    if (minY > boxHalfSize.y || maxY < -boxHalfSize.y) return false;\n\n    auto [minZ, maxZ] = minMax3(v0.z, v1.z, v2.z);\n    if (minZ > boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;\n\n    // Test triangle normal\n    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);\n    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);\n    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +\n              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +\n              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);\n    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;\n\n    // Test 9 edge cross products\n    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {\n        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);\n        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);\n        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);\n        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +\n                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +\n                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);\n        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);\n        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);\n    };\n\n    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};\n    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};\n    for (const auto& axis : axes) {\n        for (const auto& edge : edges) {\n            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);\n            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {\n                if (!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Octree for Spatial Acceleration\n// ============================================================================\n\nconstexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;\nconstexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;\n\nclass Octree {\npublic:\n    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;\n    Vec3 halfExtent, center;\n    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];\n    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list\n    const std::vector<Triangle>* allTriangles;  // Pointer to all triangles\n\n    Octree() : allTriangles(nullptr) {}\n\n    void build(const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles) {\n        allTriangles = &triangles;\n        if (triangles.empty()) return;\n\n        // Compute bounding box\n        minBound = triangles[0].a;\n        maxBound = triangles[0].a;\n        for (const auto& tri : triangles) {\n            for (const auto* v : {&tri.a, &tri.b, &tri.c}) {\n                minBound.x = std::min(minBound.x, v->x);\n                minBound.y = std::min(minBound.y, v->y);\n                minBound.z = std::min(minBound.z, v->z);\n                maxBound.x = std::max(maxBound.x, v->x);\n                maxBound.y = std::max(maxBound.y, v->y);\n                maxBound.z = std::max(maxBound.z, v->z);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Collect all indices\n        std::vector<size_t> allIndices(triangles.size());\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < triangles.size(); ++i) allIndices[i] = i;\n\n        buildNode(allIndices, minBound, maxBound);\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    void buildNode(const std::vector<size_t>& indices, const Vec3& nodeMin, const Vec3& nodeMax) {\n        minBound = nodeMin;\n        maxBound = nodeMax;\n        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;\n        center = (minBound + maxBound) * 0.5f;\n\n        // If few enough triangles or too small, make this a leaf\n        if (indices.size() <= MAX_OCTREE_TRIS ||\n            (maxBound - minBound).norm() < MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Subdivide into 8 children\n        Vec3 childHalfSize = halfExtent * 0.5f;\n        std::vector<size_t> childIndices[8];\n\n        for (size_t idx : indices) {\n            const Triangle& tri = (*allTriangles)[idx];\n\n            // Check which children this triangle overlaps\n            for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n                Vec3 childCenter = center;\n                childCenter.x += (i & 1) ? childHalfSize.x : -childHalfSize.x;\n                childCenter.y += (i & 2) ? childHalfSize.y : -childHalfSize.y;\n                childCenter.z += (i & 4) ? childHalfSize.z : -childHalfSize.z;\n\n                if (triangleBoxOverlap(childCenter, childHalfSize, tri)) {\n                    childIndices[i].push_back(idx);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Check if we can't split further (all triangles in one child)\n        bool canSplit = false;\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (!childIndices[i].empty() && childIndices[i].size() < indices.size()) {\n                canSplit = true;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (!canSplit) {\n            triangleIndices = indices;\n            return;\n        }\n\n        // Build children\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (!childIndices[i].empty()) {\n                Vec3 childMin = center;\n                Vec3 childMax = center;\n                childMin.x = (i & 1) ? center.x : minBound.x;\n                childMax.x = (i & 1) ? maxBound.x : center.x;\n                childMin.y = (i & 2) ? center.y : minBound.y;\n                childMax.y = (i & 2) ? maxBound.y : center.y;\n                childMin.z = (i & 4) ? center.z : minBound.z;\n                childMax.z = (i & 4) ? maxBound.z : center.z;\n\n                children[i] = std::make_unique<Octree>();\n                children[i]->allTriangles = allTriangles;\n                children[i]->buildNode(childIndices[i], childMin, childMax);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\npublic:\n    // Check if a ray intersects this node's bounding box\n    bool rayIntersectsBox(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2) const {\n        Vec3 d = (p2 - p1) * 0.5f;\n        Vec3 c = p1 + d - center;\n        Vec3 ad = {std::abs(d.x), std::abs(d.y), std::abs(d.z)};\n\n        if (std::abs(c.x) > halfExtent.x + ad.x) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.y) > halfExtent.y + ad.y) return false;\n        if (std::abs(c.z) > halfExtent.z + ad.z) return false;\n\n        if (std::abs(d.y * c.z - d.z * c.y) > halfExtent.y * ad.z + halfExtent.z * ad.y + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.z * c.x - d.x * c.z) > halfExtent.z * ad.x + halfExtent.x * ad.z + EPSILON) return false;\n        if (std::abs(d.x * c.y - d.y * c.x) > halfExtent.x * ad.y + halfExtent.y * ad.x + EPSILON) return false;\n\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Apply a function to all triangles potentially intersecting the ray\n    // Returns true if the function returns true for any triangle\n    template<typename Func>\n    bool applyToTris(const Vec3& p1, const Vec3& p2, Func&& func) const {\n        // If leaf node, check triangles directly\n        if (!triangleIndices.empty()) {\n            for (size_t idx : triangleIndices) {\n                if (func(idx, (*allTriangles)[idx])) return true;\n            }\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Otherwise, descend to children\n        for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {\n            if (children[i] && children[i]->rayIntersectsBox(p1, p2)) {\n                if (children[i]->applyToTris(p1, p2, func)) return true;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Mesh Generation: Icosphere\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron\nclass IcosphereMesh {\npublic:\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n\n    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {\n        // Initial icosahedron vertices\n        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;\n\n        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {\n            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,\n            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius\n        };\n\n        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)\n        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {\n            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},\n            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},\n            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},\n            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}\n        };\n\n        // Subdivide\n        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {\n            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;\n            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;\n\n            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {\n                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));\n                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);\n                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;\n\n                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;\n                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;\n                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());\n                vertices.push_back(mid);\n                midpointCache[key] = idx;\n                return idx;\n            };\n\n            for (const auto& face : faces) {\n                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);\n                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);\n                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);\n\n                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});\n                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});\n                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});\n            }\n            faces = std::move(newFaces);\n        }\n\n        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a \"room\")\n        triangles.reserve(faces.size());\n        for (const auto& face : faces) {\n            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward\n            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);\n        }\n    }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Random Number Generation\n// ============================================================================\n\nclass RandomGenerator {\n    std::mt19937 rng;\n    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;\npublic:\n    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}\n    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }\n};\n\n// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n        u = 1.0f - u;\n        v = 1.0f - v;\n    }\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n// ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];\n    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];\n\n    // Cull triangles facing the same direction\n    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle\n    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)\n    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)\n    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Initialization\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,\n                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {\n    // Generate mesh\n    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units\n    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);\n    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();\n    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;\n    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;\n\n    printf(\"Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\\n\", state.numTriangles);\n\n    // Build octree for spatial acceleration\n    printf(\"Building octree...\\n\");\n    state.octree.build(state.triangles);\n\n    // Initialize areas\n    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();\n    }\n\n    // Initialize reflectivity\n    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);\n    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);\n\n    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)\n    size_t timeOn = 0;\n    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;\n    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {\n        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Precomputation Phase\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing form factors (Kij)...\\n\");\n    RandomGenerator rng(42);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {\n            if (i == j) continue;\n            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(\n                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);\n        }\n        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {\n            printf(\"  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\\n\", i + 1, state.numTriangles);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing time delays (Tau)...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Running wave propagation simulation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n            val_t sumB = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;\n\n                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];\n                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Get radiosity from source <= ZERO) continue;\n\n                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity\n                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;\n            }\n\n state.numTimesteps) {\n            printf(\"  Timestep %zu/%zu\\n\", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n\nvoid computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"Computing distances via cross-correlation...\\n\");\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {\n        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;\n        int bestT = 0;\n\n        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {\n            val_t sum = ZERO;\n\n            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {\n                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];\n                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];\n                sum += pS * pB;\n            }\n\n            if (sum > maxCorr) {\n                maxCorr = sum;\n                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);\n            }\n        }\n\n        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);\n    }\n}\n\n// validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {\n    printf(\"\\nValidation:\\n\");\n\n    // Check that distances are non-negative\n    bool allNonNegative = true;\n    val_t minDist =
